| because | 因为那块大屏幕和环绕音响 给你的那种沉浸感，是你在家 得不到的。 | because the big screen and surround sound give you an immersive feeling you can't get at home. |
| e.g. | 而且和一群人一起看，那些笑点和惊吓 都更带劲。 | And watching with a crowd, the laughs and the scares just hit harder. |

> Mainly the atmosphere, I'd say. Because the big screen and surround sound give you an immersive feeling you can't get at home. And watching with a crowd, the laughs and the scares just hit harder.

## 老17 · An interesting building (IFS, Chengdu)　(~118s)

| [HOOK] | 所以我想聊的那栋楼 是 IFS。 | So the building I'd like to talk about is IFS. |
| [点名] | 它基本上是一个很高的购物商场，就在成都那边。 | It's basically a tall shopping mall, over in Chengdu. |
| [点名] | 而且它出名，是因为那只巨型熊猫，爬着外面那面墙的。 | And it's known for the giant panda climbing up the outside wall. |
| [平移] | 当你四处走走，有好多可看的。 | When you walk around, there's so much to see. |
| [平移 · like] | 你有那个熊猫雕塑，就像这么一只巨大的熊，挂在外墙上的。 | You've got the panda sculpture, like this huge bear hanging off the outside wall. |
| [平移] | 而且里面全是闪亮的店铺和玻璃，非常高。 | And inside it's all shiny shops and glass, really tall. |
| [FACT-DROP] | 从功能上说，它主要是个购物商场，有着一大堆商店和餐厅。 | Function-wise, it's mainly a shopping mall, with loads of shops and restaurants. |
| [FACT-DROP] | 你可以轻松地在那儿待上一整个下午，而且从顶层，你能看到很好的景，整座城市的。 | You can easily spend a whole afternoon there, and from the top floors you get a good view of the city. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | （让它出彩的那点）是 它让这座城市感觉现代，因为成都是个老城市，有着很多历史。 | What makes it stand out is it makes the city feel modern, because Chengdu is an old city with a lot of history. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 所以一栋又高又闪的楼，就在正中间，给了它一种不错的混搭，新和旧的。 | So a tall, shiny building right in the middle gives it a nice mix of old and new. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以对我来说，每当朋友们来玩，那就是一个最先去的地方之一，我带他们去的。 | So for me, whenever friends come to visit, that's one of the first places I take them. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> So the building I'd like to talk about is IFS. It's basically a tall shopping mall, over in Chengdu. And it's known for the giant panda climbing up the outside wall. When you walk around, there's so much to see. You've got the panda sculpture, like this huge bear hanging off the outside wall. And inside it's all shiny shops and glass, really tall. Function-wise, it's mainly a shopping mall, with loads of shops and restaurants. You can easily spend a whole afternoon there, and from the top floors you get a good view of the city. What makes it stand out is it makes the city feel modern, because Chengdu is an old city with a lot of history. So a tall, shiny building right in the middle gives it a nice mix of old and new. So for me, whenever friends come to visit, that's one of the first places I take them.

## 老18 · A time you needed to use your imagination — building Lego with Muye　(~118s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK] | OK 所以 我需要用到想象力的一次 是 当时我在跟我儿子木叶搭乐高。 | OK so a time I needed to use my imagination was when I was building Lego with my son Muye. |
| [CORE] | 它是 一个在家的周末，而 对我来说 它真的就归结为 瞎编乱造，没有任何说明书。 | It was one weekend at home, and for me it really comes down to making stuff up without any instructions. |
| [NARRATE · so-tag] | 所以 我们把所有积木都倒在地板上，而且 没有指南 告诉我们要搭什么。 | So we dumped all the bricks out on the floor, and there was no guide telling us what to build. |
| [NARRATE] | 所以 我们只能自己想点子，从零开始，而且 我承认，一开始 我完全不知道要搭什么。 | So we had to think of ideas on our own, from scratch, and I'll admit, at the start I had no clue what to make. |
| [NARRATE] | 然后 一旦我们上手了，它就变简单了，一边加着零碎的小块一边往下搭。 | And then once we got going, it got easier, adding bits and pieces as we went. |
| [NARRATE] | 所以 到最后 我们搭出了一种飞船 —— 比我预期的简单，其实。 | So in the end we built a kind of spaceship — easier than I expected, actually. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 原因是 我感觉真的很放松、而且意外地有创造力，因为 看到木叶的点子（这件事）激发了我自己的。 | The reason is I felt really relaxed and surprisingly creative, because seeing Muye's ideas sparked my own. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 所以 它就不再感觉像干活了，而 开始变得真正好玩。 | So it stopped feeling like work and started being properly fun. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以 对我来说，它有点 把我的童年带回来了。 | So for me, it kind of brought back my childhood. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> OK so a time I needed to use my imagination was when I was building Lego with my son Muye. It was one weekend at home, and for me it really comes down to making stuff up without any instructions. So we dumped all the bricks out on the floor, and there was no guide telling us what to build. So we had to think of ideas on our own, from scratch, and I'll admit, at the start I had no clue what to make. And then once we got going, it got easier, adding bits and pieces as we went. So in the end we built a kind of spaceship — easier than I expected, actually. The reason is I felt really relaxed and surprisingly creative, because seeing Muye's ideas sparked my own. So it stopped feeling like work and started being properly fun. So for me, it kind of brought back my childhood.

## 老19 · A person who often helps others — Zhangwei　(~118s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK] | 我想聊的那个人 是 我的朋友张伟。 | The person I'd like to talk about is my friend Zhangwei. |
| [点名 · rel-who] | 他是一个朋友，来自学校的，（那个是程序员的、跟我一样）。 | He's a friend from school, who's a programmer just like me. |
| [CORE] | 真正让他突出的那点 是 他是最乐于助人的人（我认识的）。 | What really stands out about him is he's the most helpful person I know. |
| [习惯] | 他通常做的那些事 是 帮人们弄他们的代码，把事情理顺（当有人卡住的时候），还有跟人们解释东西。 | What he usually does is help people with their code, sort things out when someone's stuck, and explain things to people. |
| [习惯] | 他就是一直坚持做。 | He just keeps at it. |
| [习惯 · so-tag] | 他一直都在做，所以他基本上就是那种人，（大家都去找的、当他们卡住的时候）。 | He does it all the time, so he's basically the kind of guy everyone turns to when they're stuck. |
| [ONE-MOMENT] | 我记得有一次我们的程序就突然不工作了，就在我们要展示它之前，然后他就坐下来开始过它。 | I remember one time our program just stopped working right before we had to show it, and he just sat down and started going through it. |
| [NARRATE-lite] | 当其他所有人都在慌的时候，他慢慢地过它，一点一点，然后把它修好了。 | While everyone else was freaking out, he went through it slowly, bit by bit, and fixed it. |
| [NARRATE-lite] | 他基本上救了场。 | He basically saved the day. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 我的感受 是 我真的很敬佩他，因为他在压力下保持冷静。 | How I feel is I really look up to him, because he stays calm under pressure. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 而对他来说，帮忙（这件事）不是一件苦差事——它是一种方式，[用来]学习，所以人们就是信任他。 | And for him, helping isn't a chore — it's a way of learning, so people just trust him. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以对我来说，说实话，每个人都需要一个朋友，像张伟这样的。 | So for me, honestly, everyone needs a friend like Zhangwei. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> The person I'd like to talk about is my friend Zhangwei. He's a friend from school, who's a programmer just like me. What really stands out about him is he's the most helpful person I know. What he usually does is help people with their code, sort things out when someone's stuck, and explain things to people. He just keeps at it. He does it all the time, so he's basically the kind of guy everyone turns to when they're stuck. I remember one time our program just stopped working right before we had to show it, and he just sat down and started going through it. While everyone else was freaking out, he went through it slowly, bit by bit, and fixed it. He basically saved the day. How I feel is I really look up to him, because he stays calm under pressure. And for him, helping isn't a chore — it's a way of learning, so people just trust him. So for me, honestly, everyone needs a friend like Zhangwei.

| because | 因为 很多孩子 在家 什么家务 都不干，所以 学校 教一教 能 补上 这一课。 | because a lot of kids don't do any chores at home, so the school teaching it can fill that gap. |
| e.g. | 比如，学校 可以 让他们 学着 自己 收拾 教室。 | For example, the school can get them to tidy up the classroom themselves. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，我 还是 觉得 这 主要 是 父母的活儿。 | Having said that, I still think it's mainly the parents' job. |

> I think that's a good idea, yeah. Because a lot of kids don't do any chores at home, so the school teaching it can fill that gap. For example, the school can get them to tidy up the classroom themselves. Having said that, I still think it's mainly the parents' job.

**Q: Why are employees reluctant to ask their managers for help?** → [REASON]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|

> Mainly it's about saving face, I'd say. Because they're scared that asking makes them look like they can't do the job, so they'd rather struggle on their own. For instance, a new employee might pretend to understand, just to make a good impression on the boss. Having said that, most bosses would actually rather you asked early.

## 老21 · A time you encouraged someone — encouraged Muye to learn swimming　(~118s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK] | 我想到的一个时候 是 我鼓励我儿子木叶去学游泳的时候。 | One time that comes to mind is when I encouraged my son Muye to learn swimming. |
| [点名 · so-tag] | 他是我儿子，而且他有点怕水，所以一开始他真的不想下水池。 | He's my son, and he was a bit afraid of water, so at first he really didn't want to get in the pool. |
| [WISH-BEAT] | 我只是想让他试一试，一点一点地，这样他就能慢慢习惯水。 | I just wanted him to give it a go, bit by bit, so he could slowly get used to the water. |
| [WISH-BEAT · to-purpose] | 所以我跟他一起下了水池，抱着他，还告诉他没什么可怕的，这样他就能有安全感。 | So I got in the pool with him and held him, and told him there was nothing to be scared of, so he could feel safe. |
| [NARRATE · so-tag] | 所以一开始他就是哭，一直紧紧抱着我，所以我差点就放弃了。 | So at first he just cried and kept clinging to me, so I almost gave up. |
| [NARRATE] | 然后我一直温柔地跟他试，一点一点地他开始放松了。 | And then I kept gently trying with him, and little by little he started to relax. |
| [NARRATE] | 最后他甚至开始享受它了，还在水里笑了。 | In the end he even started to enjoy it and smiled in the water. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 我一直坚持试的原因 是 我不想让他的害怕拖住他，因为游泳是个有用的技能。 | The reason I kept trying is I didn't want his fear to hold him back, because swimming is a useful skill. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 而且更重要的是，我想让他学到 他能战胜那些吓到他的东西。 | And more than that, I wanted him to learn he can overcome the things that scare him. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以看到他在水里笑（这件事）让这一切都值了。 | So seeing him smile in the water made it all worth it. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> One time that comes to mind is when I encouraged my son Muye to learn swimming. He's my son, and he was a bit afraid of water, so at first he really didn't want to get in the pool. I just wanted him to give it a go, bit by bit, so he could slowly get used to the water. So I got in the pool with him and held him, and told him there was nothing to be scared of, so he could feel safe. So at first he just cried and kept clinging to me, so I almost gave up. And then I kept gently trying with him, and little by little he started to relax. In the end he even started to enjoy it and smiled in the water. The reason I kept trying is I didn't want his fear to hold him back, because swimming is a useful skill. And more than that, I wanted him to learn he can overcome the things that scare him. So seeing him smile in the water made it all worth it.

**③ P3 follow-ups**

**Q: How can leaders encourage their employees?** → [ENUMERATE]

> Quite a few, actually. The biggest one, I'd say, is recognition. Because people work harder when they feel their effort is seen. For instance, a simple "well done" in front of the whole team can do more than a pay rise.

**Q: When should parents encourage their children?** → [OPINION]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 我会说，主要是当他们(正在)挣扎、快放弃的时候。 | Mainly when they're struggling and about to give up, I'd say. |
| because | 因为那(种时候)正是 一点点鼓励 影响最大的时候。 | because that's exactly when a bit of encouragement makes the biggest difference. |
| e.g. | 比如，一个孩子 数学考砸了，如果你 只是骂他，他就(会)彻底放弃。 | For instance, if a kid fails a maths test and you just tell them off, they'll give up completely. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，你不能 什么事都夸，不然它就(变得)没意义了。 | Having said that, you can't praise everything, or it stops meaning anything. |

> Mainly when they're struggling and about to give up, I'd say. Because that's exactly when a bit of encouragement makes the biggest difference. For instance, if a kid fails a maths test and you just tell them off, they'll give up completely. Having said that, you can't praise everything, or it stops meaning anything.

**Q: What kind of encouragement should parents give?** → [ENUMERATE]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|

| because | 因为光说"你真聪明" 帮不上忙——你得说 他们到底 做对了什么。 | because just saying "you're so clever" doesn't help — you have to say what they actually did right. |
| e.g. | 比如，"你这次 真的很努力"(的效果) 比"你真棒" 好，因为它 让他们知道 该重复什么。 | For instance, "you really tried hard this time" works better than "you're great," because it tells them what to repeat. |

> The best kind, I'd say, is specific praise. Because just saying "you're so clever" doesn't help — you have to say what they actually did right. For instance, "you really tried hard this time" works better than "you're great," because it tells them what to repeat.

**Q: Do you think some people are better than others at persuading?** → [OPINION]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|

| because | 因为说服 主要就是 读懂别人——知道 什么(东西)真正 打动他们。 | because persuading is mostly about reading people — knowing what actually gets to them. |
| e.g. | 有些人 天生就懂这个，而另一些人 只会 一遍遍 重复同一个论点。 | Some people just get this naturally, while others only repeat the same argument over and over. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，我觉得 这也是 能练出来的、并非纯天生。 | Having said that, I think it can be practised too, not purely born. |

> Absolutely. Because persuading is mostly about reading people — knowing what actually gets to them. Some people just get this naturally, while others only repeat the same argument over and over. Having said that, I think it can be practised too, not purely born.

**Q: Should children do everything their parents ask them to do?** → [OPINION]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |

| e.g. | 比如，如果 一个孩子 被逼着 学他讨厌的东西，他 通常 到头来 就恨上它。 | For instance, if a kid is forced to study something they hate, they usually end up hating it. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，在(涉及)安全 这类事上，他们 真的 应该听。 | Having said that, on things like safety, they really should listen. |

> Not necessarily, no. Because parents get things wrong too, and kids need to learn to think for themselves. For instance, if a kid is forced to study something they hate, they usually end up hating it. Having said that, on things like safety, they really should listen.

| e.g. | 比如，付钱 让员工 去上课 或(拿)证书，(会)让他们 想留下来。 | For instance, paying for staff to take courses or get certificates makes them want to stay. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，(如果)工资 太低，别的都白搭。 | Having said that, if the pay is too low, none of the rest matters. |

> Quite a few, and the biggest one is probably giving them room to grow. Because people stay when they feel they're going somewhere, not stuck in one place. | because | 因为这逼着你 用英语，也让你 看到 事情在全球是怎么运作的。 | Because it forces you to use English, and it shows you how things work globally. |

## 老23 · A person who likes to look after the natural world — grandfather　(~120s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK · rel-who] | 我想聊的那个人 是 我爷爷，他大概85岁、在我小时候照顾过我。 | The person I'd like to talk about is my grandfather, who's around 85 and took care of me when I was little. |
| [CORE] | 他身上真正突出的那一点 是 他有多在乎大自然。 | What really stands out about him is how much he cares about nature. |
| [平移] | 当你看他做的那些事，有很多事在进行着。 | When you look at what he does, there's a lot going on. |
| [平移 · like] | 他把他的阳台变成了一个小花园，而且有蔬菜，像西红柿、辣椒和小葱。 | He's turned his balcony into a small garden, and you've got vegetables, like tomatoes, hot peppers and spring onions. |
| [习惯 · to-purpose] | 他通常做的那些事 是 给植物浇水、把杂草拔出来，还有把厨余留起来，[用来]做他自己的堆肥。 | What he usually does is water the plants, pull out the weeds, and save kitchen scraps to make his own compost. |
| [习惯] | 他就是一直坚持着，每天做一点，而且他很小心地对待那些根。 | He just keeps at it, a bit every day, and he handles the roots with care. |
| [FACT-DROP] | 他几乎每天都在那儿——一大早，太阳一出来，他就出去给它们浇水。 | He's out there pretty much every day — first thing in the morning, as soon as the sun comes up, he goes out to water them. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 我的感受 是 我真的很敬佩他，因为他把每一株植物都当成一个家人来对待，而且现在他不工作了，这给他一种真正的平静感和目标感。 | How I feel is I really look up to him, because he treats every plant like a family member, and now he's stopped working it gives him a true sense of peace and purpose. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 跟植物待在一起（这件事）给了他一件可做的事，所以对我来说，他教会了我慢下来、[去]照顾那些小东西。 | Being around plants gives him something to do, so for me, he taught me to slow down and take care of small things. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> The person I'd like to talk about is my grandfather, who's around 85 and took care of me when I was little. What really stands out about him is how much he cares about nature. When you look at what he does, there's a lot going on. He's turned his balcony into a small garden, and you've got vegetables, like tomatoes, hot peppers and spring onions. What he usually does is water the plants, pull out the weeds, and save kitchen scraps to make his own compost. He just keeps at it, a bit every day, and he handles the roots with care. He's out there pretty much every day — first thing in the morning, as soon as the sun comes up, he goes out to water them. How I feel is I really look up to him, because he treats every plant like a family member, and now he's stopped working it gives him a true sense of peace and purpose. Being around plants gives him something to do, so for me, he taught me to slow down and take care of small things.

**③ P3 follow-ups**

**Q: Do you think parents should teach their children how to protect the environment?** → [OPINION]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|

| e.g. | 比如，我外公总是教我 别浪费水，所以现在我还是会 自动地 把水龙头关掉。 | For example, my grandpa always taught me not to waste water, so I still turn the tap off automatically. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，学校也应该帮忙——这(这件事)不只是 父母的责任。 | Having said that, schools should help too — it's not only on the parents. |

> Definitely. Because kids pick up habits early, so if they learn it young, it just sticks for life. For example, my grandpa always taught me not to waste water, so I still turn the tap off automatically. Having said that, schools should help too — it's not only on the parents.

| because | 因为当有钱牵扯进来的时候，人们(就)会守规矩，所以它真的管用。 | because people follow the rules when there's money on the line, so it actually works. |

> The biggest one's rubbish sorting, I'd say. In cities like Shanghai, you have to sort your rubbish now, and there are fines if you don't. Because people follow the rules when there's money on the line, so it actually works.

| LAND | 所以这(这件事)真的是关于 你是想让人们 在乎，还是 只是服从。 | So it's really about whether you want people to care, or just obey. |

> Most people prefer rewards, I reckon. Rewards make you want to join in, whereas punishment just makes people scared and annoyed. So it's really about whether you want people to care, or just obey.

| LAND | 话虽如此，公园 和 学校的出游 确实 帮了点忙。 | Having said that, parks and school trips do help a bit. |

> Not really, no. Because cities are all concrete and buildings, so there's not much green around. For example, a lot of city kids only see nature on a screen, or on a weekend trip. Having said that, parks and school trips do help a bit.

**Q: What can people do to protect the natural world?** → [ENUMERATE]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|

| name | 最大的那个 就是 浪费得少点——少点水、少点电、少点塑料。 | The biggest one's just wasting less — less water, less power, less plastic. |

| [FACT-DROP] | 我去过很多次，而且我现在还挺经常回去的——上一次就在不久前。 | I've been many times, and I still go back fairly often — the last time was not long ago. |

There's so much to do — you've got the food, the pandas, and its really laid-back lifestyle, like that amazing hotpot. And people sit in parks drinking tea all day. What I usually do is eat hotpot, go to see the pandas, and sit in a park, where you can just play cards with the locals. What I really enjoy is the pace of life, because people aren't always in a rush and they really know how to enjoy life. So it feels relaxed, and the food alone is worth the trip. So for me, if someone asked where to go in China, Chengdu would be one of the first places I'd recommend.

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 多了去了，说真的。 | Loads, actually. |
| name | 我会说，最大的那个是交通 —— 比如那地铁网络，(那些)把整个城市连起来的。 | The biggest one's transport, I'd say — like the subway network that connects the whole city. |
| e.g. | 除此之外，还有医院、商场、和大公园，[供]人们[去]放松。 | On top of that, there are hospitals, shopping malls, and big parks for people to relax in. |

> Loads, actually. The biggest one's transport, I'd say — like the subway network that connects the whole city. On top of that, there are hospitals, shopping malls, and big parks for people to relax in.

**Q: Do you think modern cities are suitable for young people or old people?** → [OPINION]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 主要是年轻人，我会说。 | Young people, mostly, I'd say. |
| because | 因为现代城市 节奏快、[有]很多机会，[这]很适合 年轻人，(那些)想找工作、想要社交生活的。 | because modern cities are fast-paced with lots of opportunities, which suits young people who want jobs and a social life. |
| contrast | 老人 往往更喜欢安静，所以那噪音 会让他们累。 | Older people tend to prefer quiet, so the noise can wear them out. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，好的医院 对老人来说也是个大好处。 | Having said that, good hospitals are a big plus for old people too. |

> Young people, mostly, I'd say. Because modern cities are fast-paced with lots of opportunities, which suits young people who want jobs and a social life. Older people tend to prefer quiet, so the noise can wear them out. Having said that, good hospitals are a big plus for old people too.

**Q: Before you travel to a city, what factors would you consider?** → [ENUMERATE]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 好几个呢。 | Quite a few. |
| name | 我会说，最主要的那个是安全 —— 我想知道 那地方 晚上是不是安全的。 | The main one's safety, I'd say — I want to know if the place is safe at night. |
| e.g. | 除此之外，我会看看 天气、和花费，因为这些 影响 整个行程。 | On top of that, I'd check the weather and the cost, because these affect the whole trip. |

> Quite a few. The main one's safety, I'd say — I want to know if the place is safe at night. On top of that, I'd check the weather and the cost, because these affect the whole trip.

**Q: What are the disadvantages of living in a very famous city?** → [ENUMERATE]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 我会说，最大的那个是花费。 | The biggest one's the cost, I'd say. |
| because | 因为出名的城市 房子和房租 都很贵，所以普通人 觉得 很难 存下钱。 | because famous cities have expensive housing and rent, so ordinary people find it hard to save money. |
| e.g. | 除此之外，它们 往往非常挤，尤其是[有]游客在的时候。 | On top of that, they tend to be really crowded, especially with tourists around. |

> The biggest one's the cost, I'd say. Because famous cities have expensive housing and rent, so ordinary people find it hard to save money. On top of that, they tend to be really crowded, especially with tourists around.

**Q: Do you prefer to visit well-developed cities or cities with a long history?** → [COMPARE]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 我更喜欢 有历史的那些，我觉得。 | I prefer the ones with history, I reckon. |
| 差异 | 发达的城市 全都是关于便利，而 老城市 给你 文化和故事。 | Well-developed cities are all about convenience, whereas old cities give you culture and stories. |
| LAND | 所以对我来说，它真的 主要是关于 那种氛围。 | So for me, it's really about the atmosphere. |

> I prefer the ones with history, I reckon. Well-developed cities are all about convenience, whereas old cities give you culture and stories. So for me, it's really about the atmosphere.

**Q: For those who live in cities, is it because they want to or have to?** → [REASON]

| 拍 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| STANCE | 我会说，主要是因为 他们不得不。 | Mostly it's because they have to, I'd say. |
| because | 因为好工作 和好学校 大多 在城市里，所以人们 搬去那 是为了机会，而不是为了 那生活方式。 | because the good jobs and good schools are mostly in cities, so people move there for the opportunities, not for the lifestyle. |
| concede | 当然，有些人 是真心爱 那种热闹，但我会说 那是少数。 | Sure, some people genuinely love the buzz, but I'd say that's the minority. |

> Mostly it's because they have to, I'd say. Because the good jobs and good schools are mostly in cities, so people move there for the opportunities, not for the lifestyle. Sure, some people genuinely love the buzz, but I'd say that's the minority.

## 老26 · A quiet place you like to go — quiet corner by the lake　(~115s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK] | OK 所以我想聊的那个安静的地方 是 一个小公园（离我家很近的）。 | OK so the quiet place I'd like to talk about is a small park near my home. |
| [点名] | 所以它是一个安静的小地方，就在湖边，而且它是一个（我真的很喜欢的）。 | So it's a quiet spot, right over by the lake, and it's one I really like. |
| [NARRATE-lite · dash-unpack] | 我其实是偶然发现它的 —— 有天傍晚我出去散步，然后就正好碰上了它。 | I actually found it by chance — I was out for a walk one evening and just came across it. |
| [FACT-DROP] | 我去那儿挺频繁的，尤其是当我需要清清脑子、或者暂时逃开一切一小会儿的时候。 | I go there quite often, especially when I need to clear my head or get away from everything for a bit. |
| [习惯] | 我通常在那儿做的事 是 绕着湖走走，坐在一张长椅上，然后就读读书。 | What I usually do there is walk round the lake, sit on a bench, and just read. |
| [习惯 · dash-unpack] | 没什么好赶的 —— 有时候我啥都不做，你可以就坐着看看水。 | There's no rush — sometimes I do nothing at all, you can just sit and look at the water. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 我爱它的原因 是 它太宁静了，因为你几乎听不到车流声。 | The reason I love it is it's so peaceful, because you can hardly hear the traffic. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 所以它感觉像是一个小小的逃离，从这忙碌的城市。 | So it feels like a little escape from the busy city. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以对我来说，在那儿待一段时间之后，我总是感觉更平静。 | So for me, after some time there I always feel calmer. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> OK so the quiet place I'd like to talk about is a small park near my home. So it's a quiet spot, right over by the lake, and it's one I really like. I actually found it by chance — I was out for a walk one evening and just came across it. I go there quite often, especially when I need to clear my head or get away from everything for a bit. What I usually do there is walk round the lake, sit on a bench, and just read. There's no rush — sometimes I do nothing at all, you can just sit and look at the water. The reason I love it is it's so peaceful, because you can hardly hear the traffic. So it feels like a little escape from the busy city. So for me, after some time there I always feel calmer.

| e.g. | 比如，连公园，一到周末 都挤满了人。 | For example, even parks are packed with people at weekends. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，要是你早起，清晨 还是挺安静的。 | Having said that, if you get up early, the early morning is still pretty quiet. |

> Honestly, not really. Because Chinese cities are so crowded and packed, so genuinely quiet corners are hard to find. For example, even parks are packed with people at weekends. Having said that, if you get up early, the early morning is still pretty quiet.

| e.g. | 除此之外，很多人 喜欢出去吃饭、或者跟朋友逛街。 | Apart from that, a lot of people like eating out or going shopping with friends. |

> Loads of ways, actually. But the biggest one, I'd say, is phones — people just scroll short videos and play games. Apart from that, a lot of people like eating out or going shopping with friends. Honestly, outdoor stuff isn't as popular any more.

| because | 因为(because of+名词)这些app，一切 都在你手机里，所以 你 没什么理由 出门了。 | because of all these apps, everything's on your phone, so there's not much reason to go out. |
| LAND | 话虽如此，它也让 跟远方朋友 联系 变得容易了。 | Having said that, it also makes it easy to keep in touch with faraway friends. |

> Massively, yeah. We used to go out to have fun, but now most people just stay at home on their phones. Because of all these apps, everything's on your phone, so there's not much reason to go out. Having said that, it also makes it easy to keep in touch with faraway friends.

> Not really, no. Because everyone needs to relax, not just old people. For example, young people play games and watch shows after work too. Having said that, old people do have more free time.

## 老27 · A TV/online program you like — Planet Earth (BBC, with son Muye)　(~120s)

**①拆解 · 英式中文珠子 → floor 英文**

| 拍/模式 | 英式中文珠子 | floor 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| [HOOK] | OK 那，我想聊的那个节目 是 一个自然纪录片，叫 Planet Earth，而且它是 BBC 做的。 | OK so the program I'd like to talk about is a nature documentary called Planet Earth, and it's made by the BBC. |
| [CORE] | 我爱它的那一点 是 它是那种节目之一（我永远看不腻的）。 | The thing I love about it is it's one of those shows I never get tired of. |
| [PLOT] | 它基本上 是关于 野生动物和大自然。 | It's basically about wildlife and the natural world. |
| [PLOT] | 你能看到 很棒的动物，来自全世界的，从深海到最高的山。 | You get to see amazing animals from all over the planet, from the deep ocean to the highest mountains. |
| [PLOT · which-tag] | 而酷的那一点 是 拍摄绝对惊艳，（这）总是让我惊讶。 | And the cool part is the filming is absolutely stunning, which always surprises me. |
| [FACT-DROP · rel-who] | 我通常看它 跟我儿子，牧野，（他）一看到动物就特别开心。 | I usually watch it with my son, Muye, who gets really happy when he sees the animals. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 我爱它的原因 是 画面就是很美，而且你总能学到新东西，因为它既放松又长知识，（这）很少见。 | The reason I love it is the visuals are just beautiful and you always learn something new, because it's both relaxing and educational, which is rare. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND · so-tag] | 所以 它是一个东西，（是）牧野和我能一起享受的。 | So it's something Muye and I can enjoy together. |
| [EXPLAIN=LAND] | 所以对我来说，它变成了一个小小的仪式，（是）我们俩的。 | So for me, it's become a little ritual for the two of us. |

**②整段（连起来 shadow）**

> OK so the program I'd like to talk about is a nature documentary called Planet Earth, and it's made by the BBC. The thing I love about it is it's one of those shows I never get tired of. It's basically about wildlife and the natural world. You get to see amazing animals from all over the planet, from the deep ocean to the highest mountains. And the cool part is the filming is absolutely stunning, which always surprises me. I usually watch it with my son, Muye, who gets really happy when he sees the animals. The reason I love it is the visuals are just beautiful and you always learn something new, because it's both relaxing and educational, which is rare. So it's something Muye and I can enjoy together. So for me, it's become a little ritual for the two of us.

For example, I watch lion-hunting clips with Muye — that's something you'd never see at a zoo. Having said that, going to a zoo in person feels more real.
